      :host {

        inset: var(--tooltip-top) auto auto var(--tooltip-left) !important;
        overflow: visible !important;
        text-wrap: balance;

        --tooltip-left: var(--tool-tip-position-left);

        --tooltip-top: var(--tool-tip-position-top);
        --tooltip-left: calc(var(--tool-tip-position-left, 0) - var(--overlay-offset, 0.25rem));

      :host:after{
        position: absolute;
        display: block;
        right: 0;
        left: 0;
        height: var(--overlay-offset, 0.25rem);
        content: "";

      :host(.tooltip-s):after,
      :host(.tooltip-se):after,
      :host(.tooltip-sw):after {
        bottom: 100%

      :host(.tooltip-n):after,
      :host(.tooltip-ne):after,
      :host(.tooltip-nw):after {
        top: 100%;

        from {

        to {

        animation-name: tooltip-appear;
        animation-duration: .1s;
        animation-fill-mode: forwards;
        animation-timing-function: ease-in;

        animation-duration: .1s;
        animation-fill-mode: forwards;
        animation-timing-function: ease-in;
      }

        }

</button><tool-tip id="tooltip-f7294ab3-5893-4dd3-96f3-4111a3c938d6" for="icon-button-823e9a00-1730-4d75-b433-64ac737b8c6d" popover="manual" data-direction="s" data-type="label" data-view-component="true" class="sr-only position-absolute" aria-hidden="true" role="tooltip"><template shadowrootmode="open"><style>
      :host {
        --tooltip-top: var(--tool-tip-position-top, 0);
        --tooltip-left: var(--tool-tip-position-left, 0);
        padding: var(--overlay-paddingBlock-condensed) var(--overlay-padding-condensed) !important;
        font: var(--text-body-shorthand-small);
        color: var(--tooltip-fgColor, var(--fgColor-onEmphasis)) !important;
        text-align: center;
        text-decoration: none;
        text-shadow: none;
        text-transform: none;
        letter-spacing: normal;
        word-wrap: break-word;
        white-space: pre;
        background: var(--tooltip-bgColor, var(--bgColor-emphasis)) !important;
        border-radius: var(--borderRadius-medium);
        border: 0 !important;
        opacity: 0;
        max-width: min(var(--overlay-width-small), 100vw);
        word-wrap: break-word;
        white-space: normal;
        width: max-content !important;
        inset: var(--tooltip-top) auto auto var(--tooltip-left) !important;
        overflow: visible !important;
        text-wrap: balance;
      }

      :host(:is(.tooltip-n, .tooltip-nw, .tooltip-ne)) {
        --tooltip-top: calc(var(--tool-tip-position-top, 0) - var(--overlay-offset, 0.25rem));
        --tooltip-left: var(--tool-tip-position-left);
      }

      :host(:is(.tooltip-s, .tooltip-sw, .tooltip-se)) {
        --tooltip-top: calc(var(--tool-tip-position-top, 0) + var(--overlay-offset, 0.25rem));
        --tooltip-left: var(--tool-tip-position-left);
      }

      :host(.tooltip-w) {
        --tooltip-top: var(--tool-tip-position-top);
        --tooltip-left: calc(var(--tool-tip-position-left, 0) - var(--overlay-offset, 0.25rem));
      }

      :host(.tooltip-e) {
        --tooltip-top: var(--tool-tip-position-top);
        --tooltip-left: calc(var(--tool-tip-position-left, 0) + var(--overlay-offset, 0.25rem));
      }

      :host:after{
        position: absolute;
        display: block;
        right: 0;
        left: 0;
        height: var(--overlay-offset, 0.25rem);
        content: "";
      }

      :host(.tooltip-s):after,
      :host(.tooltip-se):after,
      :host(.tooltip-sw):after {
        bottom: 100%
      }

      :host(.tooltip-n):after,
      :host(.tooltip-ne):after,
      :host(.tooltip-nw):after {
        top: 100%;
      }

      @keyframes tooltip-appear {
        from {
          opacity: 0;
        }
        to {
          opacity: 1;
        }
      }

      :host(:popover-open),
      :host(:popover-open):before {
        animation-name: tooltip-appear;
        animation-duration: .1s;
        animation-fill-mode: forwards;
        animation-timing-function: ease-in;

        max-width: min(var(--overlay-width-small), 100vw);
        word-wrap: break-word;
        white-space: normal;
        width: max-content !important;
        inset: var(--tooltip-top) auto auto var(--tooltip-left) !important;
        overflow: visible !important;
        text-wrap: balance;
      }

      :host(:is(.tooltip-n, .tooltip-nw, .tooltip-ne)) {
        --tooltip-top: calc(var(--tool-tip-position-top, 0) - var(--overlay-offset, 0.25rem));
        --tooltip-left: var(--tool-tip-position-left);

        --tooltip-left: var(--tool-tip-position-left);

        --tooltip-top: var(--tool-tip-position-top);

      }

      :host(.tooltip-e) {

        --tooltip-left: calc(var(--tool-tip-position-left, 0) + var(--overlay-offset, 0.25rem));
      }

      :host:after{
        position: absolute;
        display: block;
        right: 0;
        left: 0;
        height: var(--overlay-offset, 0.25rem);
        content: "";
      }

      :host(.tooltip-s):after,
      :host(.tooltip-se):after,
      :host(.tooltip-sw):after {
        bottom: 100%

      :host(.tooltip-n):after,
      :host(.tooltip-ne):after,
      :host(.tooltip-nw):after {
        top: 100%;
      }

      @keyframes tooltip-appear {
        from {
          opacity: 0;
        }
        to {
          opacity: 1;
        }
      }

      :host(:popover-open),

        animation-name: tooltip-appear;
        animation-duration: .1s;
        animation-fill-mode: forwards;
        animation-timing-function: ease-in;
      }

      :host(.\:popover-open) {
        animation-name: tooltip-appear;
        animation-duration: .1s;
        animation-fill-mode: forwards;
        animation-timing-function: ease-in;
      }

      @media (forced-colors: active) {
        :host {
          outline: solid 1px transparent;
        }

          display: none;
        }

System (DBMS)\u003c/strong\u003e project designed to model and manage the complete workflow of an e-commerce platform.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe system handles product catalogs, product variants, vendors, warehouses, inventory, customers, shopping carts, wishlists, orders, payments, shipments, purchase orders, reviews, coupons, and return/refund operations.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe database is implemented using \u003cstrong\u003ePostgreSQL\u003c/strong\u003e and designed with proper relationships, integrity constraints, normalization, and real-world SQL queries.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003chr\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e📌 e-commerce platforms require efficient management of multiple interconnected operations such as:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eProduct and category management\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eProduct variants\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eVendor and supplier management\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eWarehouse management\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eInventory tracking\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eCustomer management\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eShopping carts and wishlists\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eOrder processing\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003ePayment tracking\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eShipment tracking\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eCoupon management\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003ePurchase orders\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eProduct reviews\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eReturns and refunds\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eSales and revenue analysis\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThis project provides a structured relational database solution for managing these operations efficiently.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003chr\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e🛠️ Tech entities:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cmarkdown-accessiblity-table\u003e\u003ctable\u003e\n\u003cthead\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003cth\u003eEntity\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003cth\u003ePurpose\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003c/thead\u003e\n\u003ctbody\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eCategory\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eStores hierarchical product categories\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eProduct\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eStores product information\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eProductVariant\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eManages product variations such as size and color\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eVendor\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eStores supplier information\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eVendorProduct\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eConnects vendors with product variants and selling prices\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eWarehouse\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eStores warehouse details and capacity\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eInventory\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eTracks product stock across warehouses\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003ePurchaseOrder\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eStores purchase orders placed with vendors\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003ePurchaseOrderItem\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eStores products included in purchase orders\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eCustomer\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eStores customer account information\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eAddress\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eStores delivery addresses\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eCustomerAddress\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eMaps customers to multiple addresses\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eCart\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eStores customer shopping carts\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eCartItem\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eStores products added to carts\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eWishList\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eStores customer wishlists\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eWishListItem\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eStores products saved in wishlists\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eOrder\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eStores customer orders\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eOrderItem\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eStores individual items of an order\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003ePayment\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eTracks payment transactions\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eShipment\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eTracks shipment and delivery information\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eReview\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eStores customer product reviews\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eReturnRefund\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eHandles return and refund dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eA \u003cstrong\u003eCategory\u003c/strong\u003e can contain multiple Products.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eA Category can also have a parent Category.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eA \u003cstrong\u003eProduct\u003c/strong\u003e can have multiple Product Variants.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eA \u003cstrong\u003eVendor\u003c/strong\u003e can supply multiple Product Variants.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eA Product Variant can be supplied by different Vendors.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eA \u003cstrong\u003eWarehouse\u003c/strong\u003e stores multiple inventory records.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eA \u003cstrong\u003eCustomer\u003c/strong\u003e can have multiple Addresses.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eA Customer owns one Cart containing multiple Cart Items.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eA Customer can create multiple Wishlists.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eA Customer can place multiple Orders.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eAn Order can contain multiple Order Items.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eAn Order can have associated Payment and Shipment records.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eCustomers can write Reviews for purchased products.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eOrder Items can be associated with Return and Refund requests.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003chr\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e✨ Key dependency:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cblockquote\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eX → Y\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/blockquote\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003ethe determinant \u003cstrong\u003eX is a superkey\u003c/strong\u003e of the corresponding relation.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe normalization process helps reduce:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eData redundancy\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eUpdate anomalies\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eInsert anomalies\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eDelete anomalies\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe project includes a separate document containing the BCNF proof for the database 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">A comprehensive <strong>Database Management System (DBMS)</strong> project designed to model and manage the complete workflow of an e-commerce platform.</p>
<p dir="auto">The system handles product catalogs, product variants, vendors, warehouses, inventory, customers, shopping carts, wishlists, orders, payments, shipments, purchase orders, reviews, coupons, and return/refund operations.</p>
<p dir="auto">The database is implemented using <strong>PostgreSQL</strong> and designed with proper relationships, integrity constraints, normalization, and real-world SQL queries.</p>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">📌 Project Overview</h2><a id="user-content--project-overview" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 📌 Project Overview" href="https://github.com/AyushDonga/Inventory-Management-System/blob/main/README.md#-project-overview"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Modern e-commerce platforms require efficient management of multiple interconnected operations such as:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Product and category management</li>
<li>Product variants</li>
<li>Vendor and supplier management</li>
<li>Warehouse management</li>
<li>Inventory tracking</li>
<li>Customer management</li>
<li>Shopping carts and wishlists</li>
<li>Order processing</li>
<li>Payment tracking</li>
<li>Shipment tracking</li>
<li>Coupon management</li>
<li>Purchase orders</li>
<li>Product reviews</li>
<li>Returns and refunds</li>
<li>Sales and revenue analysis</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">This project provides a structured relational database solution for managing these operations efficiently.</p>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">🛠️ Tech Stack</h2><a id="user-content-️-tech-stack" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 🛠️ Tech Stack" href="https://github.com/AyushDonga/Inventory-Management-System/blob/main/README.md#%EF%B8%8F-tech-stack"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>Database:</strong> PostgreSQL</li>
<li><strong>Language:</strong> SQL</li>
<li><strong>Database Design:</strong> ER Model and Relational Model</li>
<li><strong>Normalization:</strong> Boyce-Codd Normal Form (BCNF)</li>
<li><strong>Tools:</strong> pgAdmin / PostgreSQL CLI</li>
</ul>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">🗃️ Major Database Entities</h2><a id="user-content-️-major-database-entities" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 🗃️ Major Database Entities" href="https://github.com/AyushDonga/Inventory-Management-System/blob/main/README.md#%EF%B8%8F-major-database-entities"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The database contains the following major entities:</p>
<markdown-accessiblity-table data-catalyst=""><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Entity</th>
<th>Purpose</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td>Category</td>
<td>Stores hierarchical product categories</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Product</td>
<td>Stores product information</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>ProductVariant</td>
<td>Manages product variations such as size and color</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Vendor</td>
<td>Stores supplier information</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>VendorProduct</td>
<td>Connects vendors with product variants and selling prices</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Warehouse</td>
<td>Stores warehouse details and capacity</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Inventory</td>
<td>Tracks product stock across warehouses</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>PurchaseOrder</td>
<td>Stores purchase orders placed with vendors</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>PurchaseOrderItem</td>
<td>Stores products included in purchase orders</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Customer</td>
<td>Stores customer account information</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Address</td>
<td>Stores delivery addresses</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>CustomerAddress</td>
<td>Maps customers to multiple addresses</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Cart</td>
<td>Stores customer shopping carts</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>CartItem</td>
<td>Stores products added to carts</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>WishList</td>
<td>Stores customer wishlists</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>WishListItem</td>
<td>Stores products saved in wishlists</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Order</td>
<td>Stores customer orders</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>OrderItem</td>
<td>Stores individual items of an order</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Payment</td>
<td>Tracks payment transactions</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Shipment</td>
<td>Tracks shipment and delivery details</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Coupon</td>
<td>Stores promotional coupon information</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Review</td>
<td>Stores customer product reviews</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>ReturnRefund</td>
<td>Handles return and refund requests</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">🔗 Database Relationships</h2><a id="user-content--database-relationships" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 🔗 Database Relationships" href="https://github.com/AyushDonga/Inventory-Management-System/blob/main/README.md#-database-relationships"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Some important relationships implemented in the database are:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>A <strong>Category</strong> can contain multiple Products.</li>
<li>A Category can also have a parent Category.</li>
<li>A <strong>Product</strong> can have multiple Product Variants.</li>
<li>A <strong>Vendor</strong> can supply multiple Product Variants.</li>
<li>A Product Variant can be supplied by different Vendors.</li>
<li>A <strong>Warehouse</strong> stores multiple inventory records.</li>
<li>A <strong>Customer</strong> can have multiple Addresses.</li>
<li>A Customer owns one Cart containing multiple Cart Items.</li>
<li>A Customer can create multiple Wishlists.</li>
<li>A Customer can place multiple Orders.</li>
<li>An Order can contain multiple Order Items.</li>
<li>An Order can have associated Payment and Shipment records.</li>
<li>Customers can write Reviews for purchased products.</li>
<li>Order Items can be associated with Return and Refund requests.</li>
</ul>
<hr>

<li>Track product stock across multiple warehouses.</li>
<li>Store bin locations for warehouse organization.</li>

<li>Calculate remaining warehouse capacity.</li>
</ul>

<li>Track vendor ratings.</li>

</ul>

<p dir="auto">The system tracks:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Payment gateway</li>
<li>Transaction reference</li>
<li>Payment amount</li>
<li>Payment status</li>
<li>Payment timestamp</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">Supported payment states include:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Pending</li>
<li>Success</li>
<li>Failed</li>
<li>Refunded</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">🚚 Shipment Management</h3><a id="user-content--shipment-management" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 🚚 Shipment Management" href="https://github.com/AyushDonga/Inventory-Management-System/blob/main/README.md#-shipment-management"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">🔄 Return &amp; Refund Management</h3><a id="user-content--return--refund-management" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 🔄 Return &amp; Refund Management" href="https://github.com/AyushDonga/Inventory-Management-System/blob/main/README.md#-return--refund-management"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The system supports:</p>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">🧮 Database Normalization</h2><a id="user-content--database-normalization" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 🧮 Database Normalization" href="https://github.com/AyushDonga/Inventory-Management-System/blob/main/README.md#-database-normalization"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The database schema has been analyzed using <strong>Boyce-Codd Normal Form (BCNF)</strong> principles.</p>
<p dir="auto">For every non-trivial functional dependency:</p>
<blockquote>
<p dir="auto">X → Y</p>
</blockquote>
<p dir="auto">the determinant <strong>X is a superkey</strong> of the corresponding relation.</p>
<p dir="auto">The normalization process helps reduce:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Data redundancy</li>
<li>Update anomalies</li>
<li>Insert anomalies</li>
<li>Delete anomalies</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">The project includes a separate document containing the BCNF proof for the database relations.</p>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">🔐 Data Integrity</h2><a id="user-content--data-integrity" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 🔐 Data Integrity" href="https://github.com/AyushDonga/Inventory-Management-System/blob/main/README.md#-data-integrity"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The database uses several mechanisms to maintain data consistency.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Primary Keys</h3><a id="user-content-primary-keys" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Primary Keys" href="https://github.com/AyushDonga/Inventory-Management-System/blob/main/README.md#primary-keys"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Each major entity contains a unique primary key.</p>
<p dir="auto">Examples:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><code>ProductID</code></li>
<li><code>VariantID</code></li>
<li><code>VendorID</code></li>
<li><code>WarehouseID</code></li>
<li><code>CustomerID</code></li>
<li><code>OrderID</code></li>
<li><code>PaymentID</code></li>
<li><code>ShipmentID</code></li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Foreign Keys</h3><a id="user-content-foreign-keys" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Foreign Keys" href="https://github.com/AyushDonga/Inventory-Management-System/blob/main/README.md#foreign-keys"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Foreign key constraints maintain relationships between tables.</p>
<p dir="auto">Examples:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Product → Category</li>
<li>ProductVariant → Product</li>
<li>VendorProduct → Vendor</li>
<li>VendorProduct → ProductVariant</li>
<li>Inventory → Warehouse</li>
<li>Inventory → ProductVariant</li>
<li>Order → Customer</li>
<li>OrderItem → Order</li>
<li>Payment → Order</li>
<li>Shipment → Order</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">CHECK Constraints</h3><a id="user-content-check-constraints" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: CHECK Constraints" href="https://github.com/AyushDonga/Inventory-Management-System/blob/main/README.md#check-constraints"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The database validates important business rules such as:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Vendor rating between 0 and 5</li>
<li>Positive product selling prices</li>
<li>Non-negative inventory quantity</li>
<li>Positive order item quantity</li>
<li>Valid order status</li>
<li>Valid payment status</li>
<li>Valid shipment status</li>
<li>Review rating between 1 and 5</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">UNIQUE Constraints</h3><a id="user-content-unique-constraints" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: UNIQUE Constraints" href="https://github.com/AyushDonga/Inventory-Management-System/blob/main/README.md#unique-constraints"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Unique constraints are used for attributes such as:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Product SKU</li>
<li>Variant SKU</li>
<li>Customer email</li>
<li>Customer phone number</li>
<li>Vendor GST number</li>
<li>Payment transaction reference</li>
<li>Coupon code</li>
<li>Shipment tracking number</li>
</ul>
<hr>
